These findings suggest that cunning data can strengthen model vigilance and complement conventional safety alignment.

</details>


### [152] [AeroWeaver: An Embodied-Agent Harness for Weaving Aerial Skills into Distributed, Adaptive Swarm Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18520)
*Jiabin Lou,Yirong Yang,Haopeng Wang,Xuxin Lv,Xinyu Liu,Diyuan Hou,Xuehong Liu,Rongye Shi,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AeroWeaver,一个面向无人机集群的 LLM 具身智能体框架,将个体技能编织为协同的任务级行为:通过语义决策与受控技能对接、角色条件化局部智能体实现分布式协调,并利用角色索引的"状态-动作-奖励"经验在线优化技能选择,无需训练即可实现自适应的集群智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机集群是集体智能的典型体现,而 LLM 智能体虽增强了自然语言任务理解与高层规划能力,但在应用于无人机集群时面临三大挑战:如何将模型决策落地为可执行技能、如何协调全局任务推理与分布式执行、如何利用任务经验实现持续自适应。

Method: 提出 AeroWeaver 具身智能体框架:(1) 将语义决策与受管控的技能库连接,保证决策可执行;(2) 采用角色条件化的局部智能体实现分布式协调;(3) 构建按角色索引的"状态-动作-奖励"经验库,在线优化技能选择,无需训练即可持续适应。

Result: 实验与运行时验证表明,AeroWeaver 在测试条件下能保持有效的技能执行,支持机载端多无人机协同运行(无需中心智能体基于全局上下文生成联合动作),且奖励引导的在线更新可从积累的执行经验中实现免训练的自适应学习。

Conclusion: AeroWeaver 实现了语义决策与可执行技能的有效衔接,支持无中心全局规划的分布式多机运行,并通过免训练的奖励引导在线更新,为自适应学习型无人机集群智能体提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Collective intelligence is a collaborative autonomy paradigm in which multiple agents pursue shared objectives through local perception, information exchange, and coordinated action. UAV swarms embody this paradigm by coordinating multiple vehicles in tasks such as search, inspection, and tracking. Recent advances in large language model (LLM) agents have strengthened natural-language task understanding and high-level planning, providing a flexible semantic interface between mission descriptions and collective behavior. While these advances expand semantic reasoning, applying LLM agents to UAV swarms raises challenges in grounding model decisions in executable capabilities, reconciling global task reasoning with distributed execution, and using mission-specific experience for continual adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce AeroWeaver, an embodied-agent harness that weaves individual UAV skills into coordinated mission-level behavior. AeroWeaver connects semantic decisions to governed skills, organizes role-conditioned local agents for distributed coordination, and uses role-indexed state-action-reward experience to refine skill selection online. Experiments and runtime validation show that AeroWeaver maintains valid skill execution under tested conditions and supports body-local multi-UAV operation without a central agent generating joint actions from global context, while reward-guided online updates provide a training-free path for adaptive learning swarm agents from accumulated execution experience. Code: https://github.com/Admire-ljb/AeroWeaver.

</details>


### [153] [TRIPROBE: Probing Task Separability Beyond Classification for XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18525)
*Amirhossein Sadough,Freek Hens,Aleksa Bokšan,Mohammad Mahdi Dehshibi,Mahyar Shahsavari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriProbe 是一个用于可解释诊断任务可分性的多层级探测框架。它将多任务问题分解为二元子任务，并在输入空间（基础探测）、特征表示（潜在探测）和分类器输出（最终探测）三个层面追踪可分性演化，以最大 Fisher 判别比作为度量，定位瓶颈及受影响的任务对，并在 Roshambo sEMG 基准上验证了其揭示隐藏失效、指导数据收集与架构设计的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习管线的评估往往退化为单一的下游准确率指标，无法回答任务为何成功或失败这一根本问题。将模型视为黑盒使得缺乏可解释的诊断手段来定位可分性瓶颈，因此需要一个能够沿数据处理层级追踪任务可分性的系统性诊断框架。

Method: TriProbe 将多任务问题分解为二元子任务，并在三个互补层面应用探测：作用于输入空间的基础探测、作用于特征表示的潜在探测、作用于分类器输出的最终探测。整个框架以最大 Fisher 判别比作为有原则的可分性度量指标，追踪可分性如何在输入、学习到的特征与最终分类器之间逐层演化，从而实现可解释的瓶颈定位。

Result: TriProbe 能够识别可分性瓶颈及受影响的任务对。在 Roshambo sEMG（表面肌电信号）基准上的实验表明，该框架可以揭示隐藏的失效模式，进而为数据收集、验证策略和模型架构设计提供有针对性的指导。

Conclusion: TriProbe 表明，评估学习系统不应止步于单一的下游准确率指标。通过对输入空间、特征表示与分类器输出三个层面的系统性探测，并配合最大 Fisher 判别比这一有原则的可分性度量，该方法能够揭示传统评估中被掩盖的失效模式，为数据收集、验证流程与架构设计等实践环节提供可操作的指导，推动评估从"黑盒打分"转向"可解释诊断"。

Abstract: Modern evaluation of learning pipelines often reduces to downstream accuracy, leaving open the question of why tasks succeed or fail. TriProbe addresses this gap with a multi-level probing framework for explainable diagnosis of task separability. Rather than treating models as black boxes, TriProbe traces how separability evolves across inputs, learned features, and final classifiers. It decomposes multi-task problems into binary subtasks and applies three complementary probes: a Foundational Probe on input spaces, a Latent Probe on feature representations, and a Final Probe on classifier outputs. Using Maximum Fisher's Discriminant Ratio as a principled separability metric, TriProbe identifies bottlenecks and affected task pairs. Experiments on the Roshambo sEMG benchmark show how TriProbe reveals hidden breakdowns, guiding data collection, validation, and architecture design.

</details>


### [154] [Recursive Reasoning or Statistical Extrapolation? In-Context Learning in Multi-Agent Interdependent Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18591)
*Yu Liu,Wenwen Li,Yifan Dou,Guangnan Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究 LLM 智能体在多智能体不完全信息博弈中的上下文内学习(ICL)本质:究竟是递归信念的策略性推理,还是对历史统计模式的外推。通过公共品博弈实验并操纵历史反馈的统计结构,以理性预期均衡(REE)为基准,发现一旦历史统计模式被打乱,长上下文的收益几乎消失,决策质量退化至无上下文基线,且在策略相互依赖性更强时退化更严重——表明此类环境中的 ICL 更符合统计外推而非策略性推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文内学习(ICL)使大语言模型(LLM)智能体能够利用交互历史改进决策,但这种改进究竟源于内部策略性推理(如递归信念推理)的精细化,还是仅仅对历史统计模式的外推,尚不清楚。厘清这一机制对于理解 LLM 智能体在策略性环境中的真实能力至关重要。

Method: 作者构建了一个公共品博弈作为不完全信息多智能体环境,系统性地操纵历史反馈的统计结构,并以与历史无关的理性预期均衡(REE)作为基准来评估智能体的决策质量,从而将"利用历史统计模式"与"进行策略性推理"两种机制解耦并分别检验。

Result: 实验发现,当历史统计结构被破坏(扰动)后,更长上下文带来的收益基本消失,决策质量退化至无上下文基线水平,且这种退化在策略相互依赖性更强的环境中被显著放大。这表明 ICL 的表现主要依赖历史统计规律,而非稳健的策略性推理能力。

Conclusion: 该研究表明,在不完全信息多智能体博弈中,LLM 智能体的上下文内学习更接近统计外推而非策略性推理。其贡献在于:将 ICL 机制研究扩展至策略性多智能体环境;提出以理性预期均衡(REE)作为区分推理与外推的诊断工具;并提供了一个可复用的实验框架,用于探测 LLM 在递归信念任务中的推理边界。这对评估 LLM 在真实博弈与经济交互场景中的可靠性具有警示意义。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables large language model (LLM) agents to improve decisions using interaction history, yet it remains unclear whether such improvement reflects refined internal reasoning or mere extrapolation of statistical patterns. To disentangle these mechanisms, we study LLM agents in multi-agent incomplete-information games that require recursive belief reasoning. By constructing a public goods game and manipulating the statistical structure of historical feedback, we evaluate decision quality against a history-independent rational expectations equilibrium (REE) benchmark. Our experiments reveal that when historical statistical patterns are disrupted, the benefits of longer context largely vanish, degrading decision quality to the no-context baseline in a way sharply amplified by stronger strategic interdependence. These results suggest that, in such strategic environments, ICL behavior is more consistent with statistical extrapolation than with strategic reasoning. Our work extends the mechanistic study of ICL to strategic multi-agent settings, introduces REE as a diagnostic tool for distinguishing reasoning from extrapolation, and provides a reusable framework for probing the boundaries of LLM reasoning in recursive belief tasks.

</details>


### [155] [Reasoning through Evolution: Automatic Meta-path Discovery for LLM-based Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18597)
*Ziyi Zhou,Xiaoming Zhang,Hui Pang,Yuting Zhang,Tiesunlong Shen,Bingyu Yan,Erik Cambria,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MAGER——一个多智能体遗传进化框架,自动搜索面向大语言模型(LLM)推理优化的元路径,将复杂传播图压缩为信息子图,并结合图上下文学习检索语义与结构相似的示例,使冻结的 LLM 在零样本/少样本场景下成为强大的假新闻检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传播结构是假新闻检测的关键证据,但现有方法主要依赖有监督的 GNN 模型,需要大量标注数据且泛化能力有限;而大语言模型虽具备强推理能力,直接输入原始传播图会造成显著的模态失配与严重的信息过载,导致其在零样本和少样本场景下难以进行可靠的"结构感知"推理。因此需要一个桥梁,让冻结的 LLM 能够以低成本、数据高效的方式利用传播结构。

Method: 提出 MAGER 框架:(1) 采用多智能体遗传进化机制,自动搜索并演化出面向 LLM 推理优化的元路径,将复杂的传播图压缩为信息丰富且紧凑的子图,从而缓解信息过载与模态失配,使冻结的 LLM 无需训练即可执行结构感知的真伪推理;(2) 引入图上下文学习策略,检索语义与结构上均相似的示例作为示范,以强化 LLM 的分类与推理能力。代码已开源。

Result: 大量实验表明,在数据高效(零样本/少样本)的设置下,MAGER 能够显著提升冻结 LLM 作为独立假新闻检测器的性能,实现了可靠的结构感知推理。

Conclusion: 本文表明,无需微调、仅通过进化搜索得到适配 LLM 推理的元路径,加上融合语义与结构信息的上下文示例检索,就能让冻结的大语言模型在标注数据稀缺的场景下有效利用传播结构进行结构感知的真伪推理,显著提升其作为独立假新闻检测器的性能,为低资源条件下结合传播结构与 LLM 推理能力提供了高效可行的范式。

Abstract: Propagation structures provide crucial evidence for fake news detection, yet existing approaches primarily rely on supervised GNN-based models, which require substantial labeled data and exhibit limited generalization. Although large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, directly feeding them raw propagation graphs creates a significant modality mismatch and severe information overload, making structure-aware reasoning unreliable in zero-shot and few-shot settings. To bridge this gap, we propose MAGER, a multi-agent genetic evolution framework that automatically discovers meta-paths optimized for LLM reasoning. By compressing complex propagation graphs into informative subgraphs, the evolved meta-paths alleviate both information overload and modality mismatch, enabling frozen LLMs to perform structure-aware veracity reasoning. We further introduce a graph in-context learning strategy that retrieves semantically and structurally similar demonstrations to strengthen classification and reasoning. Extensive experiments show that MAGER substantially improves frozen LLMs as standalone fake news detectors in data-efficient settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/SenticNet/MAGER.

</details>


### [156] [The Uneven Impact of Generative AI on Student Learning: Examining the Roles of Reliance, Evaluation Literacy, and Course Policy in AI-related Courses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18676)
*Lydia Manikonda,Mei Si,Sirajam Munira,Oshani Seneviratne,Kristin Bennett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于某院校12门AI相关课程118名学生的问卷调查，研究将GenAI使用者分为四类聚类，并发现学术收益与早期依赖、学术任务支持相关；负面影响与早期依赖、态度改变相关，且评估素养越高，早期依赖带来的负面影响越强。免费/付费版本、工具数量及教师政策均导致学习体验差异，研究呼吁机构制定更完善的政策以应对AI使用中的不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能（GenAI）正在改变学生的学习方式，但课程情境、认知依赖、评估素养与早期依赖等因素在其学习体验中的作用尚未被充分探究。作者旨在考察学生对GenAI的使用差异及感知学习体验的差异，识别影响学习收益与负面体验的关键因素，为机构层面的AI政策制定提供实证依据。

Method: 对118名修读12门AI相关课程的学生进行问卷调查；采用聚类分析识别GenAI用户群；比较免费版与付费版、单一工具与多工具、不同教师政策下的使用与体验差异；构建多变量回归模型，以早期依赖、认知依赖、学术任务支持、对GenAI可靠性的信心、态度改变、评估素养等为自变量，分别预测学术收益、正面影响与负面影响，并检验评估素养的调节（交互）作用。

Result: 共识别出四类用户群：高使用组报告收益最多；轻度使用组依赖较少、收益也较少；两个中度使用组报告的收益水平不同。免费版与付费版用户、单一工具与多工具用户、以及经历不同教师政策的学生之间存在显著差异。回归模型显示：学术收益与早期依赖、学术任务支持相关；正面影响与认知依赖、学术任务支持、对GenAI可靠性的信心及教师政策相关；负面影响与早期依赖、态度改变相关；且评估素养越高，早期依赖与负面影响的关联越强。学生对GenAI增强学习的感知体现为认知、表现与自我效能方面的收益，而对压力增加和批判性思维减弱的担忧与较低的学习收益感知相关。

Conclusion: 早期依赖对学习体验是把双刃剑：既与学术收益相关，也与负面影响相关，且评估素养越高其负面关联越强。使用条件（版本、工具数量、教师政策）造成了学习体验的不平等。机构需制定更完善的政策与指导规范，缩小这类数字与教学情境带来的差距，使学生能够公平地从日益强大的AI系统中受益。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) is changing how students learn, yet the roles of course context, cognitive reliance, evaluation literacy, and early reliance remain underexplored. Using survey responses from 118 students across 12 AI-related courses at our institution, we examined differences in GenAI use and perceived learning experiences. We identified four user clusters: high-use students reporting many benefits, light users reporting less reliance and fewer benefits, and two moderate-use groups reporting different levels of benefit. We also found significant differences between free- and premium-version users, single- and multiple-tool users, and students experiencing different instructor policies. In multivariable regression models, academic benefit was associated with early reliance and academic task support; positive impact was associated with cognitive reliance, academic task support, confidence in GenAI reliability, and instructor policy; and negative impact was associated with early reliance and attitudinal change. The association between early reliance and negative impact became stronger as evaluation literacy increased. Finally, perceptions of GenAI-enhanced learning appear to reflect cognitive, performance, and self-efficacy benefits, while concerns about stress and diminished critical thinking are associated with lower perceived learning benefits. These findings suggest that institutions need better policies to address such inequities so that institutions can enable students to benefit from increasingly capable AI systems.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Truncation: Rethinking LLM Decoding as Ensemble Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18723)
*Dunyao Xue,Chengshuo Du,Zhengbo Wang,Wenlin Dai,Cheng Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出马氏距离集成解码（ME-Decoding），将 LLM 解码中的候选词元选择建模为"集成剪枝"问题：利用基于词元嵌入的自适应带宽核构建相似度矩阵，以马氏距离驱动的目标函数对冗余生成路径进行动态折扣，在保持高概率的同时提升语义多样性；并设计带早停的贪心选择算法，实现候选规模下的近线性复杂度与理论近似保证，是即插即用、开销可忽略的解码模块，在多种推理与生成任务上表现稳定优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 解码中的候选词元选择策略主要依赖标量概率，忽视了词元之间的几何语义关系，导致候选之间存在大量语义冗余；而现有的几何感知方法要么需要复杂的优化过程，要么直接对原始词元概率进行重加权，带来显著的计算开销或推理不稳定性。因此亟需一种既利用语义几何信息、又高效稳定且开销可忽略的解码方法。

Method: 1) 将解码建模为候选子集优化问题，以马氏距离驱动的目标函数同时增强语义多样性并保留高概率词元；2) 通过在词元嵌入上构建自适应带宽核函数生成词元相似度矩阵，动态对冗余生成路径进行折扣（惩罚），从而在集合层面剪枝冗余候选；3) 设计带早停机制的贪心选择算法，使复杂度在候选规模下接近线性，并为其建立了理论近似保证（近似比界）；4) 整个方法作为即插即用模块嵌入现有 LLM 解码流程，推理开销可忽略。

Result: 在多样化的推理与生成任务上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法能够持续取得强劲的性能表现，同时由于具备近线性复杂度与早停机制，其推理开销几乎可以忽略，验证了方法在效果与效率上的双重优势。

Conclusion: ME-Decoding 将候选词元选择建模为基于马氏距离的子集优化问题，通过自适应带宽核构建相似度矩阵来动态折扣冗余生成路径，并以具备近似理论保证的贪心算法实现近线性复杂度的高效求解，形成了一个无需复杂优化、开销极低的即插即用解码模块，实验证明其在推理与生成任务上均能带来一致的性能提升。

Abstract: We introduce Mahalanobis-Ensemble Decoding (ME-Decoding), a novel Large Language Model (LLM) decoding framework that frames candidate token selection as ensemble pruning. Existing selection strategies rely predominantly on scalar probabilities, ignoring geometric semantic relationships and causing candidate redundancy. Meanwhile, current geometry-aware methods often require complex optimization or directly reweighting the original token probabilities, leading to significant computational overhead or inference instability. To address this, we formulate decoding as a subset optimization problem using a Mahalanobis distance-driven objective to enhance semantic diversity while preserving high probabilities. Specifically, we dynamically discount redundant generation paths using a token similarity matrix, constructed via an adaptive-bandwidth kernel over token embeddings. We further devise an efficient greedy selection algorithm with near-linear complexity in the candidate size under early stopping, while establishing its theoretical approximation guarantees. This renders ME-Decoding a robust, plug-and-play module with negligible inference overhead. Extensive experiments across diverse reasoning and generation tasks demonstrate that our method consistently achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [158] [Which LLM is Best for Translating Natural Language Goals to PDDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18731)
*Tomas Balyo,Lukas Chrpa,G. Michael Youngblood*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实证评估了六个主流大语言模型将视频游戏测试员用非正式自然语言编写的测试目标转换为规范PDDL形式化目标的能力。通过精心设计的提示模板和真实世界领域基准测试，所有模型正确率均超过92%：Gemini 2.5 Flash以96%准确率及最低假阳性率居首，GPT-4.1在响应速度上领先。研究表明LLM在连接自然语言与自动规划方面已取得显著进展，但仍受语言歧义和领域表达能力限制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动规划领域，用户需以PDDL等形式化语言表达目标，这对非专家用户而言门槛很高，导致人类意图与机器执行之间存在鸿沟。本文的动机在于实证检验当前的大语言模型能否可靠地将视频游戏测试员以非正式自然语言书写的测试目标，翻译成适用于经典规划器、格式正确的PDDL目标，从而探索LLM作为自然语言与自动规划流水线之间桥梁的可行性。

Method: 研究方法包括：（1）通过迭代实验提炼出一个精心设计的提示模板，以同时最大化输出准确性与响应一致性；（2）选取六个当代最先进的大语言模型进行系统性评估；（3）构建基于真实世界的、领域特定的基准数据集（由游戏测试员以非正式语言编写的测试目标）；（4）从正确性、响应速度和错误倾向三个维度对各模型进行量化比较分析。

Result: 评估结果显示：所有模型均展现出高正确率，超过92%；其中Gemini 2.5 Flash以96%的准确率位居第一，且假阳性（误报）发生率最低；GPT-4.1则在响应速度上领先。尽管整体表现优异，各模型之间仍存在关键的性能差异，且偶发性失败主要源于自然语言的歧义性以及领域表示能力的局限。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：当前最先进的LLM在将自然语言游戏测试目标转换为PDDL这一特定任务上已能达到很高的正确率（>92%），表明LLM有潜力充当自然语言与自动规划流水线之间的有效桥梁，降低非专家使用形式化规划工具的门槛。然而，模型间在精度与速度上仍存在关键差异，且语言歧义与领域表达能力不足导致的偶发失败说明该技术尚未完全成熟，距离成为稳健可靠的转换工具仍有差距，需要在歧义消解和领域表示方面继续改进。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between human intent and machine execution remains a challenge in automated planning, where expressing goals in formal languages like PDDL restricts accessibility to non-experts. This paper empirically evaluates whether current Large Language Models (LLMs) can reliably translate natural language testing goals, written in informal language by video game testers, into well-formed PDDL targets suitable for classical planning. We present a carefully designed prompt template, integrating insights from iterative experimentation, aimed at maximizing both accuracy and response coherence from multiple state-of-the-art LLMs. Six contemporary models are systematically assessed on correctness, speed, and error tendencies using real-world, domain-specific benchmarks. All models demonstrate high correctness, exceeding 92\%, with Gemini 2.5 Flash achieving the highest accuracy at 96\% and the lowest incidence of false positives, while GPT-4.1 leads in response speed. Despite these advances, critical distinctions exist in model performance, and occasional failures arise from language ambiguity and limitations in domain representation. Our analysis underscores both the significant progress and ongoing gaps in enabling LLMs to act as robust bridges between natural language objectives and automated planning pipelines.

</details>


### [159] [Clueing up LLMs with Tool-Augmented Deductive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18736)
*Rebecca Ansell,Autumn Toney-Wails*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将经典桌游《妙探寻凶》改编为文本化多智能体环境，用于评估大语言模型在多轮、代理式演绎推理中的表现。作者以 GPT-4o-mini 和 Gemini-2.5-Flash 各三个智能体进行轮次制对局建立基线，并引入一种工具增强方法：通过结构化"可能性矩阵"将推理日志中的隐式博弈状态转化为显式的剩余可能性表示，外化扩展记忆与演绎约束，从而提升推理质量与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型近期进展显著，但在长交互中执行逻辑一致的演绎推理仍然困难。需要在多个推理步骤间整合证据、与先前推断保持一致并在新约束下更新信念的任务，能够暴露当前模型的局限性，同时为评估推理增强提供有效测试平台。现有评测缺乏对多步、代理式演绎推理的动态、对抗性检验场景，因此作者需要一个结构化、可验证的多智能体推理环境。

Method: 作者构建了文本化的多智能体版《妙探寻凶》游戏环境，智能体需从连续观测序列中推断隐藏信息、跨回合保持一致性，并对不断演化的逻辑约束集进行推理。实验实例化六个基于 LLM 的玩家（GPT-4o-mini 与 Gemini-2.5-Flash 两个模型家族各三个），进行多轮重复对局以建立基线性能。随后提出工具增强方法：用结构化可能性矩阵从生成的推理日志中提取隐式博弈状态，转化为显式的剩余可能性表示，该矩阵编码扩展回合记忆与演绎约束，将这些认知负担从智能体本身卸载（offloading），并与基线方法进行对比评估。

Result: 研究建立了两种模型家族在重复对局中的基线表现，揭示了智能体在跨回合一致性与多步约束整合方面的不足。通过可能性矩阵的工具增强方法，将隐式的记忆维护与约束推理显式化后，智能体在推理质量和任务成功率上相较基线得到改善，表明外部化表示有助于缓解 LLM 在长程演绎推理中的认知负荷问题。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：《妙探寻凶》这类结构化博弈环境可有效检验 LLM 的多步、代理式演绎推理能力；显式化的可能性矩阵作为工具增强手段，通过外化记忆与逻辑约束，能显著提升自主智能体在战略推理环境中的推理质量与成功率，为推理增强型智能体的评测与改进提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), performing logically consistent deductive reasoning over extended interactions remains challenging. Tasks that require integrating evidence across multiple reasoning steps, maintaining consistency with prior inferences, and updating beliefs under new constraints can surface limitations in current models while providing a useful testbed for evaluating reasoning enhancements. In this paper, we implement a text-based, multi-agent version of the classic board game Clue as an environment to evaluate multi-step, agentic deductive reasoning. In this setting, agents must infer hidden information from a sequence of observations, maintain consistency across turns, and reason over an evolving set of logical constraints. We instantiate six LLM-based agents (GPT-4o-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash) as players that engage in turn-based gameplay; using three agents per model family, we establish baseline performance across repeated games. We then introduce a tool-augmented approach in which a structured possibility matrix converts implicit game state from generated reasoning logs into an explicit representation of remaining possibilities. The possibility matrix encodes extended-turn memory and deductive constraints, offloading these tasks from the agent. We compare this approach against the baseline to evaluate how tool augmentation supports reasoning quality and task success for autonomous agents in a strategic reasoning environment.

</details>


### [160] [Version- and Scope-Aware Question Answering over Normative Documents: A Deployed System and an End-to-End Evaluation at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18769)
*Liuyin Wang,Shuaipeng Jin,Jiwei Shi,Jensen Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了"上传文档即可提问"的托管检索服务在规范性文档问答中的可靠性：基于生产环境中约7.3万份候选规范性文档，从公开基准分层抽取200道均带黄金源文档的问题，对比托管服务与在生成前通过显式规则解析版本与适用范围的受治理系统。受治理系统得分97.7，托管服务仅88.1，差距9.6分。该受治理配置自2026年1月起商业化运营，服务1126名注册用户（客户包括智谱AI与乐程健康），至2026年4月中旬每工作日处理约10万次调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正确回答以规范性文档（法规、规章、政策等）为依据的问题，通常依赖于任何单一文本片段之外的信息：检索到的文档是否为现行有效版本；它是否适用于所涉辖区、主体（如机构或申请人）与日期；以及每条规范性论断能否溯源到其依据文本。托管检索服务大幅降低了构建此类系统的初始工程成本，使"上传文档、直接提问"成为常见默认做法，但该默认做法在版本有效性、适用范围与溯源要求上的可靠性缺乏系统评估，因此需要实证检验其是否足以支撑规范性问答任务。

Method: 评估基于生产部署所提供的约73,000份候选规范性文档。从已发布基准中按分层抽样抽取200个问题，每题均有黄金源文档；公开的抽样规则不读取任何系统输出或分数，避免选择偏差。对比两种系统：托管检索服务（上传即问的默认方案）与受治理系统（在生成之前通过显式规则解析文档版本与适用范围）。对两系统使用相同的答案文本进行评分，并公开问题集、被评测的答案文本、分数以及复现基准统计的脚本。

Result: 受治理系统总体得分97.7，托管服务得分88.1，两者差距9.6分（由未舍入的均值计算得出）。问题集、两系统被评测的答案文本、分数及用于复现基准统计的脚本均已公开，结果可独立验证。

Conclusion: 对于以规范性文档为依据的问答，"上传即问"的托管检索默认方案在版本有效性、适用范围与可溯源等方面不足以满足要求，与显式规则治理的系统存在9.6分的显著差距；在生成前通过显式规则解析版本与适用范围能显著提升答案可靠性。该受治理配置自2026年1月起作为商业产品运营，服务1126名注册用户（客户包括智谱AI与乐程健康），至2026年4月中旬达到每工作日约10万次调用，表明该治理式方法不仅性能更优，也具备实际生产可行性与商业落地能力。

Abstract: Correctly answering a question grounded in normative documents often depends on information outside any single passage: whether the retrieved document is the version currently in force; whether it applies to the jurisdiction, subject (such as an institution or applicant), and date at issue; and whether each normative claim can be traced to its supporting source text. Hosted retrieval services have substantially lowered the engineering cost of building an initial system over such corpora, making "upload the documents and ask" a common default. We evaluate this default on approximately 73,000 candidate normative documents supplied to a production deployment. The evaluation uses a stratified sample of 200 questions from our published benchmark, with a gold source document for every question; the released sampling rule reads no system outputs or scores. We compare the hosted service with a governed system that resolves version and scope through explicit rules before generation. The governed system scored 97.7 overall, while the hosted service scored 88.1, a gap of 9.6 points computed from unrounded means. The question set, the answer text evaluated for both systems, the scores, and the scripts used to reproduce the reported benchmark statistics are public. The governed configuration has operated as a commercial product since January 2026 and serves 1,126 registered users; named customer organizations include Zhipu AI and Lecheng Health. By mid-April 2026, it had reached roughly 100,000 calls per workday.

</details>


### [161] [CERA-MoA: Co-Evolving Routing Mechanisms with Continually Learning LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18779)
*Jiaxuan Jiang,Liyuan He,Zhixuan Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CERA-MoA，一种协同进化强化学习框架，将动态路由器与持续学习的智能体策略联合优化。通过基于中隐层状态的熟悉度估计器预测智能体语义胜任能力，并采用累积阈值自适应路由激活最小智能体子集，同时根据智能体能力演化主动分配定向训练样本，实现性能与效率的平衡，实验显示其超越现有静态路由与固定流程微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合智能体范式通常将查询路由与智能体微调视为两个相互独立的过程：路由策略无法在智能体后训练过程中随其能力演化而调整，智能体也难以通过数据驱动的专门化训练与路由机制形成协同。这种脱节限制了整个多智能体系统对动态变化的能力分布的响应能力，因此需要一种能将路由与训练耦合、共同进化的框架。

Method: CERA-MoA 采用迭代式强化学习框架，使动态路由器与独立智能体策略协同进化。方法包含三个核心设计：(1) 预测式熟悉度估计器——利用模型中隐层状态评估各智能体对查询的语义胜任度，避免完整推理带来额外开销；(2) 累积阈值自适应路由机制——根据熟悉度分数动态激活定制化的最小智能体子集，在任务性能与效率之间取得平衡；(3) 能力导向的样本分配策略——依据智能体不断演化的能力，主动为其分配针对性训练样本，促进能力差异化分工。

Result: 在多个领域的广泛实验中，CERA-MoA 的表现优于当前最先进的静态智能体路由方法与固定工作流微调基线，验证了路由与智能体协同进化设计的有效性，同时自适应路由机制在效率与性能之间实现了良好的折中。

Conclusion: 本文的核心结论是：将查询路由与智能体微调统一到同一强化学习框架中实现协同进化，能够克服静态路由与固定流程微调的固有局限。通过基于中隐层状态的语义能力估计、累积阈值自适应路由以及基于能力演化的定向样本分配，CERA-MoA 在保持推理效率的同时获得了更优的任务性能，为多智能体系统中"路由—训练"联合优化提供了有效的范式参考。

Abstract: Current Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) paradigms generally treat query routing and agent fine-tuning as separate processes, limiting their ability to respond to evolving agent capabilities. This disconnect prevents routing strategies from adapting to evolving agent capabilities during post-training and prevents agents from achieving synergistic data-driven specialization. To resolve this, we introduce CERA-MoA (Co-Evolving Router with continually learning Agents for Mixture-of-Agents), an iterative reinforcement learning framework where the dynamic router and independent agent policies co-evolve. We design a predictive familiarity estimator that leverages mid-layer hidden states to evaluate semantic competence among agents, avoiding the overhead of full rollouts. Based on these familiarity scores, a cumulative-threshold adaptive routing mechanism dynamically activates a tailored minimal agent subset, achieving a trade-off between task performance and efficiency. By proactively allocating targeted training samples to agents based on their evolving competence, CERA-MoA promotes capability differentiation. Extensive experiments across various domains demonstrate that CERA-MoA outperforms state-of-the-art static-agent routing and fix-workflow fine-tuning baselines.

</details>


### [162] [Compositional Policy Violations: When Step-Level Compliance Fails In Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18820)
*Ashwini Kurady,Sri Sai Charith Grandhi,Rajesh Gupta,Sumit Mamoria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，当前的智能体工作流治理几乎全部是"步骤级"的（如输入输出分类器、逐轮护栏、片段级评估器），而组织实际持有的政策（转诊阈值、权限限制、审查要求等）却是整个执行的属性而非单一步骤的属性。这种错位导致了一类新型失败——"组合式政策违规"（Compositional Policy Violation, CPV）：每一步都通过了各自的检查，但组合后的完整执行却违反了总体政策。由于单步谓词无法评估该步骤本身不决定的属性，因此无论把步骤级监视器做得多准，都无法检测这一失败类别。论文将CPV形式化定义为"步骤级合规无法组合"的失败，提出四类分类法（权限蔓延、阈值洗白、累计总和违规、上下文坍塌），指出每类的正确修复方式由被守护量在何处发生变异所决定，并进一步提出一种溯源感知的运行时架构：在完整执行轨迹上评估政策，并从原始溯源数据而非流水线的派生表示中重新计算被守护量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于：智能体工作流如今在受监管环境中做出具有重大影响的决策，但围绕它们的治理机制几乎完全是"步骤级"的——输入输出分类器、每轮护栏、片段级评估器。然而组织实际持有的政策（如转诊阈值、权限限制、审查要求）是整个执行的属性而非任何单一步骤的属性。这种范围错位会导致一种此前未被充分形式化的失败模式：单步谓词无法评估该步骤本身并不决定的属性，因此无论怎样提升步骤级监视器的精度，都无法检测这一类失败。论文旨在识别、定义并修复这一治理缺口。

Method: 方法上，论文首先将CPV形式化定义为"步骤级合规性无法组合"的失败模式，并构建了一个四类分类法：（1）权限蔓延、（2）阈值洗白、（3）累计总和违规、（4）上下文坍塌。其核心分析工具是"被守护量的变异位置"：每类CPV的正确修复方式都由该被守护量在执行过程中的哪一步发生变异所决定。基于这一洞见，论文提出一种溯源感知的运行时架构：不再信任流水线各步派生的表示，而是在完整执行轨迹上评估政策，并从原始溯源数据重新计算被守护量，从而在全局层面判断组合执行是否合规。

Result: 结果包括三个层面：其一，论证了CPV是一类步骤级监视器在原理上无法检测的失败——这是结构性的盲区而非精度问题，"更准的单步检查"对该类别无效；其二，给出了CPV的四类分类法，并证明每一类的正确修复由被守护量的变异位置所唯一决定，为修复策略的选择提供了系统性判据；其三，提出了溯源感知的运行时架构，通过在完整执行轨迹上从原始溯源重新计算被守护量，使整体性政策可以被正确评估和执行。

Conclusion: 论文的结论是：步骤级合规在原理上不可组合，任何仅提升单步监视器精度的努力都无法消除CPV这一结构性盲区。有效的政策治理必须从"步骤级"范式转向"轨迹级"范式：在完整执行轨迹上评估政策，并基于原始溯源数据而非流水线派生表示重新计算被守护量。这一架构转变使组织真正持有的整体性政策（权限、阈值、审查要求）能够在智能体工作流中被可靠地强制执行。

Abstract: Agentic workflows now make consequential decisions in regulated settings, and the governance placed around them is almost entirely step-scoped: input-output classifiers, per turn rails, and span-level evaluators. The policies organizations actually hold, such as referral thresholds, authority limits, and review requirements, are properties of the whole execution rather than of any one step. This mismatch admits a failure mode we call a Compositional Policy Violation (CPV): every individual step passes its own check while the composed execution violates the governing policy. A predicate over a single step cannot evaluate a property that step does not determine, so no improvement in the accuracy of the step-scoped monitors detects this class. We define CPVs as the failure of step-level compliance to compose, and present a taxonomy of four types: Authority Creep, Threshold Laundering, Cumulative Sum Violation, and Context Collapse. We show that the correct repair for each class is dictated by where the guarded quantity mutates. We then introduce a provenance-aware runtime architecture that evaluates policies over complete execution traces, recomputing guarded quantities from raw provenance rather than the pipeline's derived representation.

</details>


### [163] [Infinite-Parameter LLMs: Generating and Adapting Weights from Live Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18842)
*Jinli Hu,Ross M. Clarke,Yichuan Zhang,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"无限参数LLM"(Infinite-Parameter LLM):借助一个紧凑超网络,把运行时交互数据(用户提供的事实、纠正等)转化为对共享基础网络的低秩调制,即前馈权重由实时数据动态生成而非固定存储;不同于以往"读一次上下文即冻结"的权重生成方法,它在生成器潜在编码上维护贝叶斯信念并在线更新,使有效权重随会话推进持续重新推导。由此,模型存储足迹固定,但可编译的权重近乎无限,能将实时知识写入权重而非提示词,实现摊销计算、释放上下文窗口、跨轮次持久化并可能获得更好的泛化;论文同时给出与上下文学习和检索增强对比的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律与MoE架构的成功均建立在静态预训练数据之上,但部署后的模型面对的是另一个世界:许多能提升其效用的知识并不在训练集中,而在当前正在处理的实时交互中,例如用户临时提供的事实或给出的纠正。由于传统模型训练后权重冻结,无法从这些数据中学习,现有做法只能将此类知识以检索或指令形式放入提示词,在每次请求时重新读取、请求结束后即被丢弃。由此,论文提出的核心问题是:能否设计一种架构,使其能够从实时交互中学习并将这些知识直接写入权重?

Method: 受MoE"只激活部分参数"思想启发,提出**无限参数LLM**,核心技术路线有三:(1) **低秩权重生成**:用一个紧凑超网络将运行时输入数据转化为对共享基础网络的低秩调制,前馈权重由实时数据按需生成,而非存储于固定参数库中;(2) **贝叶斯在线信念更新**:与以往"读取一次上下文即冻结权重"的权重生成器不同,该方法在生成器的潜在编码上维护一个贝叶斯后验信念,并随会话进程在线更新,有效权重从这一持续演化的信念中重新推导,从而实现会话内的累积学习与纠错;(3) **评估协议设计**:明确规定了将该权重内学习方式与上下文学习及检索增强方法进行对比检验的实验方案。

Result: 作为摘要,本文未报告具体实验数字,而是陈述了该设计的预期收益与核心主张:将运行时知识承载于权重而非提示词,可以(1)摊销计算成本(知识编码一次、后续复用,而非每轮重读);(2)释放宝贵的上下文窗口;(3)知识跨轮次持久保留;(4)相比上下文内使用,可能取得更好的泛化效果。同时,论文的关键形式化贡献是:在存储足迹保持固定的情况下,模型可编译的权重空间实际上是无限的,即以有限存储逼近"无限参数"的表达能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个清晰的架构愿景与方向:将"运行时学习"从提示词层面下沉到权重层面,以贝叶斯在线信念驱动的权重生成实现会话内持续学习,而不增加部署后的存储成本。其概念贡献在于把运行时知识的吸收形式化为"信念更新驱动的动态权重编译",并给出了与上下文学习和检索增强对标的评估协议。需要注意的是,摘要未呈现实验数据,评估协议亦仅为"指定"而非已验证,故该工作更像立场/框架性论文或早期探索,其关键主张(跨轮持久性、泛化优势、计算摊销)仍待实证检验;此外,贝叶斯信念在线更新的稳定性、超网络容量瓶颈及实际部署开销也是后续需解决的开放问题。

Abstract: The scaling laws hold that a language model grows more capable with more parameters and more training data, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have ridden these laws to remarkable results, activating only a fraction of an enormous stored parameter bank for each token. That success is built on static pretraining data. A deployed model faces a different world, where much of the data that would make it more useful is not in its training set but in the live interaction it is currently handling, such as the facts a user supplies or the corrections they give. A conventional model cannot learn from this data, because its weights are frozen after training. Instead, the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time are placed in the prompt, by retrieval or instruction, and re-read on every request only to be discarded once the request ends. We ask how an architecture could learn from live interaction by writing it into its weights. Taking inspiration from MoE, we propose the \textbf{Infinite-Parameter LLM}. A compact hypernetwork turns the data given at run time into a low-rank modulation of a shared base network, so the feed-forward weights are generated from live data rather than stored in a fixed bank. Where prior weight generators read the context once and freeze, we carry a Bayesian belief over the generator's latent code and update it online, so the effective weight is re-derived from that evolving belief as the session proceeds rather than fixed after one read. The stored footprint stays fixed, yet the weights the model can compile are effectively infinite. For the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time, carrying them in the weights rather than the prompt is amortized in compute, frees the context window, persists across turns, and can generalise better than in-context use. We specify an evaluation protocol that tests exactly this against in-context learning and retrieval.

</details>


### [164] [Suppressed, Not Erased: A Representational Trace of Edited Facts Survives Even Weight-Free Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18985)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用线性探针直接测量知识编辑后模型内部的"残余知识"：在GPT-2-XL上对50条CounterFact编辑，即使三种机制不同的编辑方法（ROME、约束微调、GRACE）在生成层面均100%成功，原始事实对象在隐藏状态中仍远高于随机水平（0.50）地线性可解码（ROME 0.96、约束微调0.86、GRACE 0.79）。尤其是GRACE不改任何基座权重仍出现残迹，说明编辑只是"抑制"而非"擦除"表示空间中的原有关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：现有知识编辑基准只认证"局部正确性"——即编辑后的模型在接近编辑提示的输入上能否生成新事实——却不回答一个关键问题：原始事实在模型内部还残存多少、是否仍可被解码。由于残余知识关系到模型遗忘、隐私删除与安全性的真实性评估，作者希望绕开行为层面的表面指标，直接从内部表征测量编辑后的残余知识，并判断该残迹究竟源于权重更新不完整，还是编辑本身在表示层面的根本局限。

Method: 方法：采用"线性轨迹探针"作为核心工具——在完成一条事实编辑后，训练线性分类器从模型隐藏状态中判断原始对象是否仍可恢复。实验设置：在GPT-2-XL上，对50条CounterFact编辑，分别应用三种机制上截然不同的编辑器：(1) ROME（定位-编辑式权重更新）、(2) 约束微调、(3) GRACE（不改基座权重、通过外部记忆覆盖事实的记忆式编辑器）。同时以生成层面的编辑成功率（100%）作为行为正确性基线，并尝试用"再学习节省"（relearning savings）作为残余知识的辅助测量手段。

Result: 结果：(1) 三种编辑方法在生成层面均达到100%成功，但原始对象在隐藏状态中仍远高于随机水平（0.50）地线性可解码：ROME探针准确率0.96、约束微调0.86、GRACE 0.79。(2) 最具信息量的是GRACE结果——它不改动任何基座权重，仅靠外部记忆覆盖事实，但原对象仍可从底层网络解码，因此残余痕迹不能归因于权重更新不完整。(3) 再学习节省工具在本文设置中表现不可靠，作者将其作为阴性方法学结果报告，而非作为证据使用。

Conclusion: 结论：知识编辑即使行为上完全成功，也只是在表示空间中抑制而非擦除原始关联；残余痕迹并非权重更新不完整的产物，而是旧知识仍内在于模型表征的证据。这一发现表明，仅依赖生成层面的评估不足以判定事实的"删除/遗忘"是否彻底，对模型遗忘、隐私保护与安全审计具有直接方法论意义。作者同时明确指出，再学习节省指标在本设置中不可靠，应视为阴性方法学结果而非证据，避免过度解读。代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Knowledge-editing benchmarks certify local correctness, whether an edited model produces the new fact on near-edit prompts but not how much of the original fact remains decodable inside the model. We study residual knowledge directly with a linear trace probe: after editing a fact, we ask whether the original object is still recoverable from the model's hidden states. On GPT-2-XL, across three mechanistically distinct editors applied to 50 CounterFact edits, the original object remains linearly decodable well above chance after a successful edit (probe accuracy 0.96 for ROME, 0.86 for constrained fine-tuning, and 0.79 for the memory-based editor GRACE, against a chance level of 0.50; all edits reach 100% generation-based success). The GRACE result is the most informative: GRACE changes zero base-model weights, overriding the fact through an external memory, yet the original object is still decodable from the underlying network, so the residual trace cannot be attributed to an incomplete weight update. We read this as evidence that editing, even when behaviorally successful, suppresses rather than erases the original association in representational space. We also report a relearning-savings instrument that did not behave reliably in our setting and discuss why; we treat it as a negative methodological result rather than evidence. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [165] [Function Lives Where Variance Doesn't: Task-Weighted Charts of a Language Model's Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18989)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"任务加权图"(task-weighted charts)框架,将"语言模型计算实际使用多少维度"这一病态问题良定义化:针对某个选定的表示泛函,在其自身度量下用低维坐标系拟合,把蒸馏转化为普通最小二乘。在三个家族、70M至7B参数的六个模型上发现:维持下一词预测困惑度在完整模型5%以内需要残差流宽度的70–90%,且该宽度由语言的稀有尾部消耗;而激活方差与此几乎无关——GPT-2中两个方向承载90%的激活方差却几乎不承载功能。维度是"依泛函而异"的:模型自身的不确定性只需六个坐标即可读取,而完整预测分布需要数百维,且维度随深度增长。在只能保留少量维度时,按泛函度量训练的图式比基于方差或最优线性压缩更能保持模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "语言模型的计算实际使用了多少维度?"这一问题在指明所针对的泛函之前是病态的。以往对模型内在维度的刻画多依赖激活方差(如PCA),但方差大小并不等于功能重要性,可能导致对维度需求的错误估计。作者希望给出一个良定义的维度概念,并回答:维持模型特定功能输出所需的维度到底有多少?它与方差结构是否一致?

Method: 提出"任务加权图"框架:针对所选的表示泛函,在其自身度量下拟合低维坐标系,从而把蒸馏/降维问题转化为普通的最小二乘问题。在来自三个模型家族、参数量从70M到7B的六个语言模型上,以不同泛函(如完整的下一词预测分布、模型自身的不确定性等)为目标,测量保持该泛函输出在给定精度内所需的最小维度,并与基于方差(如PCA/最优线性压缩)的方法进行对照。

Result: 1) 跨越70M至7B参数、三个家族的六个模型中,下一词预测需要残差流宽度的70–90%才能将困惑度维持在完整模型的5%以内;这一宽度由语言的稀有尾部词汇所消耗。2) 方差分布完全无法预测功能维度:GPT-2中两个方向承载了90%的激活方差,却几乎不承载任何功能。3) 维度是"依泛函而异"的:模型自身的不确定性只需六个坐标即可读取,而完整预测分布需要数百维;维度随深度增长。4) 当只能保留少量维度时,在泛函自身度量下训练的图式比基于方差或最优线性压缩的方案更好地保持模型预测。

Conclusion: 语言模型计算的"有效维度"不是一个固定数字,而是相对于所选泛函而言的属性;方差大小不能代理功能重要性,且二者可以严重脱节。这为理解表示学习提供了新视角:功能几何与统计几何需区分对待;同时为蒸馏、低秩近似与高效推理提供了实用准则——按目标任务泛函度量训练的低维映射优于基于方差的通用压缩方法。维度随深度增长也提示深层网络逐步利用更宽泛的功能子空间,对机制可解释性研究具有方法论意义。

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [166] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Dhruvkumar Patel,Ria Khatoniar,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个横跨物理与几何基准的五任务诊断实验,将多模态大模型在科学推理中的失败解耦为感知问题或推理问题。研究发现:错误的图表理解会拖累即使纯文本就能答对的问题;准确率从原始图像到人工字幕普遍上升;部分模型在修正字幕后恢复率高,以此可区分"感知受阻"与"真正的推理瓶颈";感知失败后的错误类型因领域而异(物理多为计算错误,几何多为概念误用)。此外,InternS1-mini 虽经大量科学预训练,却在所有任务上低于最弱基线模型,且推理轨迹频繁提前截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机:现有多模态大语言模型在科学推理基准上报告了亮眼成绩,但大多数基准把"感知(图像理解)"与"推理"当作单一可测量的整体过程,导致无法判断模型失败究竟是"看不懂图"还是"推不出来"。这种混淆阻碍了对模型真实能力的精确诊断与针对性改进,因此需要一套能将感知失败与推理失败分离开的诊断方法。

Method: 方法:作者构建了横跨物理与几何基准的五任务诊断实验,通过受控的输入模态对比(纯文本、原始图像、人类撰写的字幕、修正后的字幕等条件)对同一组问题进行测试,从而将模型失败解耦并归因于感知、推理或两者兼有;同时考察模型在修正字幕下的表现恢复情况,并分析感知失败后所引发的推理错误类型。

Result: 结果:1) 错误的图表理解会降低性能,即使是那些模型仅凭文本就能正确解决的问题也会受拖累;2) 准确率总体上随输入从原始图像过渡到人类撰写的字幕而逐步上升;3) 部分模型在修正字幕下表现出很高的恢复率,据此可将"感知受阻型失败"与"真正的推理瓶颈"区分开来;4) 感知失败后出现的推理错误类型取决于领域:物理域的失败多归为计算错误,几何域则多归为概念误用;5) 作为核心实验之外的讨论,InternS1-mini 尽管进行了大量科学领域预训练并具备思考能力,却在所有任务上低于实验中最弱的模型,且其推理轨迹经常在完成前被截断。

Conclusion: 结论:科学推理基准必须将感知与推理解耦评估,否则高估或误判模型能力。修正字幕是一种有效的诊断工具,可区分感知受阻型失败与真正的推理瓶颈。感知失败所引发的下游推理错误具有领域特异性,针对性改进应分领域施策。此外,科学预训练并不保证多模态科学推理能力,像 InternS1-mini 这类模型反而暴露出全任务落后与推理截断等缺陷,说明还需在感知—推理协同与长程推理稳定性上做更多工作。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>


### [167] [Compiled Agency: Frontier General-Purpose Coding Agents Build Winning Game Players from Bare Interaction - from Flappy Bird to StarCraft II and Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18996)
*Joey Xiao,Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Gauntlet框架（开发-冻结-评估），让通用编码智能体仅凭游戏描述与裸接口自主构建完整游戏控制器，冻结后在保留实例上零模型调用评估。结果显示最新一代智能体在新roguelike上成功率达0–86%且呈代际跨越，并首次在《星际争霸II》击败全部公平内置AI、在Freeciv中完成全面征服，验证了"编译智能体"能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM智能体在游戏中屡屡难以将知识转化为高水平对局（即使配备感知、记忆、技能库、规划器等脚手架），编码智能体却进步迅速。由此提出两个尖锐问题：前沿模型现在能否真正赢得游戏？能否在无任何辅助下自己构建整个玩家程序？

Method: Gauntlet框架：仅向通用编码智能体提供游戏描述、原始观察/动作接口和空策略文件，智能体在单次自主会话中试玩并编写独立控制器；随后冻结程序，在保留测试实例上零模型调用地评估。测试对象包括未公开的程序化roguelike、完整《星际争霸II》API控制器及文明类游戏Freeciv。

Result: 在未公开的程序化roguelike上，保留集成功率为0–86%，并呈现明显的代际门槛：最新一代系统的所有会话均优于前代最佳会话。在全游戏尺度上，编译出的原始API控制器击败所有公平的《星际争霸II》内置AI及两个作弊变体；单会话程序在保留种子上以全面征服赢得完整文明类游戏。虽然对新手AI胜率不高，但这是语言智能体首次无需逐轮模型调用和手工战术层即独立赢得该类型完整游戏。

Conclusion: 论文提出"编译智能体"概念：开发经验被编译为持久的可执行智能体，其架构由模型自身构建。前沿编码智能体开始具备追踪长程策略的能力，这是通用语言智能体在完整游戏自主对局上的首次突破。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [168] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MUSE——一个用于评估大型视觉语言模型在情境化教育应用中艺术图像理解能力的基准。MUSE 通过解耦图像标注与问题生成来降低标注成本并实现难度可控，涵盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解、组合推理四大维度共十二项任务，并聚焦新加坡与东南亚多元文化语境及西方艺术传统。评测发现各模型能力差异显著，尤其在情感解读与组合推理方面薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在多模态理解方面取得显著进展，但其在教育场景中的能力仍缺乏充分评估。在 AI 辅助语言学习中，模型需解读艺术图像、理解其语义、情感与文化内涵，并进行视觉情境推理以支持有意义的交互。然而现有基准主要聚焦真实世界图像或特定领域的教育推理，对艺术教育内容的覆盖十分有限，因此亟需专门的评测基准填补这一空白。

Method: MUSE 基准采用"图像标注与问题生成解耦"的设计方法，在降低标注成本的同时实现任务多样性与难度可控。基准包含十二项任务，覆盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解及组合推理四大能力维度；图像数据经过精心策划，以新加坡及东南亚多元文化语境为核心，并涵盖西方艺术传统，涉及多种主题与难度层级。

Result: 对开源与专有模型的评测显示，各能力维度之间存在显著性能差异，尤其在情感解读与组合推理方面表现薄弱。进一步分析识别出模型常见的失效模式以及构建可信教育多模态模型所面临的关键挑战。

Conclusion: MUSE 为情境化教育应用中的多模态理解研究提供了一个标准化评测基准，通过系统揭示现有模型的失效模式与关键挑战，推动可信教育多模态模型的发展。作者期望 MUSE 能成为该领域的重要参考基准，促进面向教育场景的多模态理解能力提升。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [169] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"旗帜博弈"(Flag Game)这一玩具模型,用于研究多智能体系统中集体信念形成与传播的机制。研究发现:小规模群体易发生集体信念崩溃,而大规模群体则转向集体信念极化,后者导致性能下降但带来信念多样性;同时,社会意识提示、团队多样性与组织结构显著影响表现。作者提出"社会电路归因"(social circuit attribution)技术与统计力学理论两条互补路径剖析机制,并以此为"群体机制可解释性"(mechanistic swarm interpretability)奠定第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的涌现性协调行为正日益构成关键安全风险,其核心驱动因素是关于世界的信念在群体中的快速形成与传播。然而,目前缺乏对这一集体现象的机制性理解,而机制层面的剖析是实现"集体对齐"(collective alignment)的前提。因此,作者需要一个可控、可分析的最小实验平台来研究集体信念形成的底层机制。

Method: 作者构建了Flag Game玩具模型:由一个隐藏的国家旗帜定义真值,每个有界理性智能体只能直接观察私人证据(作物),但可与同伴交换信念并权衡社会证据的权重。在此基础上:(1) 系统变化种群规模、提示方式、团队构成与组织结构,刻画集体现象学;(2) 提出"社会电路归因"技术,预测哪个智能体及其何种观点对集体动态最关键,并通过因果干预(智能体修补/patching)验证其预测;(3) 由于因果干预效力随群体规模增大而衰减,进一步发展适用于大群体的统计力学理论,并将其与经验相图对照验证。

Result: Flag Game尽管简单,却复现了丰富的集体现象学:(1) 性能随种群规模呈非单调变化;(2) 社会意识提示与团队多样性可提升准确率;(3) 组织结构具有强烈影响。关键发现是:小规模群体中出现集体信念崩溃,随群体增长该现象转变为集体信念极化;极化导致大群体性能下降,但同时产生了集体信念的多样性。机制分析表明:社会电路归因能预测关键智能体与关键观点,并经因果干预验证,但其效力随群体扩大而下降;此时统计力学理论与经验相图吻合,补全了大群体情形的解释。

Conclusion: 该论文以极简的Flag Game为载体,系统揭示了AI智能体集体信念形成的机制性规律:群体规模决定了集体失效模式(小群体崩溃、大群体极化),而社会证据的传播结构是核心杠杆。其提出的"群体机制可解释性"将机制可解释性从单个智能体内部扩展到智能体群体层面,并示范了从微观因果干预到宏观统计力学理论的两级分析范式,为集体对齐、群体安全和可控的多智能体系统设计提供了理论基础与方法论起点。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>


### [170] [Cognitive Extensions for Dual-Process Language Agents: Memory and Self-Reflection in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19128)
*João Meneses dos Santos,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在双过程语言代理SwiftSage基础上,加入自适应记忆模块(AMM)与自我反思模块(SRM)两个认知扩展,并在ScienceWorld上做受控消融实验。完整系统取得最优成绩(平均分64.62、成功率43.17%、成功步效率19.33步),其中SRM是贡献最大的独立组件,说明执行时控制是当前性能的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言代理在交互式环境中仍然脆弱:任务成功需要长时程状态追踪、合法动作执行以及从失败步骤中恢复。现有方法在执行时缺乏对动作有效性的验证机制,且缺少能被恰当触发的记忆检索,因此亟需模块化的认知扩展来系统性地提升可靠性。

Method: 在SwiftSage(快系统动作提议者+慢系统规划器)基础上,以特征开关模块化扩展方式实现两个认知模块:自适应记忆模块(AMM)负责显著性门控的情景存储与触发式检索;自我反思模块(SRM)负责有界的执行时验证与纠错干预。两模块共享同一执行基底,并在ScienceWorld基准上设置四种配置进行受控消融实验:基线、基线+AMM、基线+SRM及完整系统。

Result: 完整系统在四组配置中表现最佳:平均最终得分64.62、成功率43.17%、成功步效率19.33步。消融实验显示SRM是最强的单一贡献模块,而AMM的增益依赖于已稳定的运行回路;表明执行时控制是该场景下的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该研究表明,在交互式环境中,执行时控制(自我反思与纠错)是提升语言代理性能的首要瓶颈,应优先投入;情景记忆的增益依赖稳定的运行回路,适合作为第二步增强。模块化的特征开关设计支持可控消融,为认知增强组件的独立评估提供了范例。

Abstract: Language agents remain brittle in interactive environments, where success requires long-horizon state tracking, valid action execution, and recovery from failed steps. We extend SwiftSage, a dual-process agent that combines a fast action proposer with a slower planner, using two modular cognitive extensions: an Adaptive Memory Module (AMM) for salience-gated episodic storage and trigger-driven retrieval, and a Self-Reflection Module (SRM) for bounded execution-time validation and corrective intervention. Both modules are implemented as feature-flagged extensions over the same execution substrate, enabling controlled ablations on ScienceWorld. Across four configurations---baseline, baseline+AMM, baseline+SRM, and the full system---the full system achieves the best mean final score (64.62), success rate (43.17%), and successful-step efficiency (19.33 steps), while SRM is the strongest standalone contributor. The results suggest that execution-time control is the dominant bottleneck in this setting, while episodic memory becomes most useful once the runtime loop is stabilized.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Scaling Articulated Rationales for MLLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17639)
*Haoke Xiao,Yueyang Liu,Yuhui Zhang,Xiang Chen,Yufei Liu,Jia Xu,Yalong Guan,Xiaolan Zhu,Xiaoyu Zhang,Shijun Wang,Shuang Yang,Zijie Meng,Zejian Zhang,Ruochen Yang,Xiangyu Wu,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Lantao Hu,Cheng Luo,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 SARA 工业框架，将快手直播 2.4 亿用户的自然语言偏好解释（AUR，显性化用户理由）转化为可扩展的推荐信号。通过数据引擎构建高质量数据集 SARA-HQ、用 SFT + Quality-Refining DPO 训练 SARA-7B 将理由生成从 8.6 万作者扩展至 1000 万作者空间、再由 SARA-Ranker 集成进生产排序，线上 A/B 测试显示互动提升且负反馈下降，证明 AUR 可成为工业推荐系统的一等文本信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统主要依赖点击、观看时长、负反馈等隐式行为信号，但这类信号只能揭示用户"做了什么"，无法回答用户"为什么"喜欢或不喜欢内容。本文提出显性化用户理由（AUR）——即用户用自然语言对自身偏好的解释——作为一类全新的、具有极性感知和原因层级的文本推荐信号。然而 AUR 天然稀疏、质量参差不齐、且仅覆盖极小部分物品，难以直接应用于工业系统，因此需要一套系统化框架来将其规模化。

Method: SARA 框架分三个阶段：(1) 数据引擎：从快手直播 2.4 亿用户中诱导并筛选 AUR，构建经过质量控制、以作者为中心的高质量数据集 SARA-HQ；(2) SARA-7B：通过大规模 SFT 与 Quality-Refining DPO 对通用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）进行对齐，将理由生成能力从 86,564 位 AUR 覆盖作者扩展至全部 1000 万作者空间；(3) SARA-Ranker：通过理由感知交互建模与拒绝记忆建模，将生成的正、负理由集成进生产环境排序模型。

Result: 广泛的离线评估、人工校准与在线 A/B 测试表明：SARA-7B 生成的理由相比强 MLLM 基线更加具体、极性一致且有据可依；SARA-Ranker 在生产环境中显著提升用户参与度并降低负反馈。系统以每日刷新方式稳定部署超过 30 天，验证了该框架的工程可行性与长期有效性。

Conclusion: SARA 成功将显性化用户理由确立为工业推荐系统中实用的一等文本信号。该工作表明：通过数据引擎+生成模型扩展+排序集成的完整链路，稀疏低质的自然语言偏好解释可以被转化为可规模化、可持续每日刷新的生产级信号，为推荐系统从"行为级"信号迈向"原因级"信号提供了经过大规模线上验证的可行范式。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems largely infer user preferences from implicit behaviors such as clicks, watch time, and negative feedback, but these signals reveal what users do rather than why they like or dislike content. This work studies articulated user rationales (AURs), i.e., users' natural-language explanations of their preferences, as a new class of polarity-aware and reason-level textual signals for recommendation. Despite their potential value, AURs are difficult to use in industrial systems because they are naturally sparse, often low-quality, and only cover a small fraction of items. We present SARA (Scaling Articulated Rationales), an industrial framework that turns sparse AURs into scalable recommendation signals. SARA first builds a data engine that elicits and curates AURs from 240M Kuaishou Live users, producing SARA-HQ, a quality-controlled and author-centric rationale dataset. It then aligns a general-purpose MLLM into SARA-7B through large-scale SFT and Quality-Refining DPO, extending rationale generation from 86,564 AUR-covered authors to the full 10M-author space. Finally, SARA-Ranker integrates the generated positive and negative rationales into production ranking via rationale-aware interaction modeling and rejection-memory modeling. Extensive offline evaluation, human calibration, and online A/B tests show that SARA-7B generates more specific, polarity-consistent, and grounded rationales than strong MLLM baselines, while SARA-Ranker improves engagement and reduces negative feedback in production. Deployed with daily refresh for over 30 days, SARA establishes articulated rationales as a practical, first-class textual signal for industrial recommendation systems.

</details>


### [172] [One Size Does Not Fit All! Dynamic Retriever and Generator Selection for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17709)
*Neeraj Anand,Payel Santra,Partha Basuchowdhuri,Debasis Ganguly,Sumit Bhatia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DRAG，一个按查询自适应选择“检索器-生成器”配置的 RAG 框架。研究发现检索与生成的增强收益均呈递减且非单调趋势，固定配置并非最优。DRAG 包含免训练的 DRAG_QPP（基于查询性能预测与困惑度信号路由）和监督微调的 DRAG_SFT 两种方法，在三个 LLM 家族与四个问答基准上，前者在显著降低推理延迟的同时保持与静态强基线相当的性能，后者则持续提升效果，整体实现了优于静态 RAG 的效果-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统通常对所有查询采用固定的检索器和生成器配置，但查询的复杂度和信息需求差异巨大，导致计算资源分配低效。此前检索自适应与生成自适应已被独立研究，但二者联合对端到端 RAG 性能的影响仍缺乏系统探索，因此需要系统分析检索器与生成器复杂度之间的交互作用。

Method: 作者提出 DRAG——一个按查询自适应选择“检索器-生成器”配置的框架，包含两种路由方法：(1) DRAG_QPP：免训练路由，利用查询性能预测信号指导检索器选择，并基于检索上下文的困惑度度量指导生成器选择；(2) DRAG_SFT：监督路由，通过微调 LLM 使其联合预测检索器-生成器配置。实验涵盖三个 LLM 家族和四个问答基准，涉及事实型与多跳问答（含桥接与组合推理任务）。

Result: 分析表明：(1) 更强的检索通常比增加生成算力带来更大收益，但二者均呈现收益递减且非单调的特性，说明更高复杂度的配置并非对所有查询一致更优。实证上，DRAG_QPP 在大幅降低推理延迟的同时取得与强静态 RAG 基线相当的性能；DRAG_SFT 则持续优于静态基线和免训练自适应基线，效果更佳。

Conclusion: 本文的结论是：检索与生成的联合自适应配置优于静态 RAG 流水线，能够实现更有利的“效果-效率”权衡。研究证实更高复杂度的配置并非在所有查询上一致更优，因而根据查询特性动态选择“检索器-生成器”组合是优化端到端 RAG 系统的有效路径：无训练的 QPP 路由适合追求低延迟与低成本的部署场景，监督微调路由则适合追求效果上限的场景。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems typically employ fixed retriever and generator configurations across queries, despite substantial differences in query complexity and information needs, leading to inefficient allocation of computational resources. While retrieval and generation adaptivity have been studied independently, their joint effect on end-to-end RAG performance remains underexplored. We systematically analyze how retriever and generator complexity interacts across factoid and multi-hop question answering (QA), including bridge and composition reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that stronger retrieval generally yields larger gains than increased generation effort, but both exhibit diminishing and non-monotonic returns, indicating that higher-complexity configurations are not uniformly better across queries. Motivated by these findings, we introduce DRAG, a query-adaptive framework for selecting retriever-generator configurations. We first propose DRAG$_\text{QPP}$, a training-free routing approach that uses Query Performance Prediction (QPP) signals to guide retriever selection and perplexity-based measures over retrieved context to guide generator selection. We further introduce DRAG$_\text{SFT}$, a supervised routing approach that fine-tunes an LLM to jointly predict retriever-generator configurations. Across three LLM families and four QA benchmarks, \qpprag~achieves performance comparable to strong static RAG baselines while substantially reducing inference latency, whereas DRAG$_\text{SFT}$ consistently improves effectiveness over static and training-free adaptive baselines. Overall, DRAG demonstrates that jointly adapting retrieval and generation achieves a more favorable effectiveness-efficiency trade-off than static RAG pipelines.

</details>


### [173] [DUPAR: Dual-Path Conversational Retrieval via Speech Retriever with Cross-Turn Evidence Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18042)
*Yuanjun Li,Yiwen Liu,Dapeng Li,Zhiwei Xu,Bin Zhang,Shengtao Zhang,Rong Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 DUPAR，一种面向语音助手的对话式检索框架。针对"ASR 转写 + 文本检索"级联带来的延迟与错误传播，以及直接语音检索的跨模态失配问题，DUPAR 设计了互补的快慢双路径：快路径用与冻结 BGE-M3 文本嵌入对齐的任务自适应音频编码器在跨轮证据缓存中检索，置信度不足时慢路径融合音频与 ASR 转写嵌入进行全索引检索，并通过一跳图扩展刷新下一轮证据缓存。在领域知识库上，该方法在干净语音下接近文本检索精度，查询侧较 ASR+文本编码器加速 3.75 倍；噪声基准 Recall@10 从 0.771 提升至 0.875，多种合成说话风格下整体 Recall@1 提升 4.2 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于外部知识的语音助手普遍采用"ASR 转写 + 文本检索"的级联流程，该流程引入额外延迟，且语音识别错误会向下游检索传播；而直接以语音查询进行检索又容易受到音频与文本模态间的语义失配（跨模态失配）影响。因此需要一种兼顾低延迟、识别噪声鲁棒性与多轮对话上下文利用的语音检索框架。

Method: DUPAR 采用双路径互补架构：1) 快路径：训练一个任务自适应的音频编码器，将其嵌入与冻结的 BGE-M3 文本嵌入对齐，直接在跨轮证据缓存中检索；2) 慢路径：当缓存检索置信度不足时，融合音频嵌入与 ASR 转写文本嵌入进行全索引检索，提升鲁棒性；3) 缓存维护：慢路径选中的证据通过知识库图结构的一跳扩展，刷新生成下一轮的证据缓存，从而复用对话历史与图结构信息。

Result: 在特定领域知识库上的实验表明：1) 训练后的音频编码器在干净语音上的检索精度接近文本检索，且查询侧速度较"ASR + 文本编码器"级联方案加速 3.75 倍；2) 在噪声基准上平均 Recall@10 从 0.771 提升至 0.875；3) 在多种合成说话风格下，整体 Recall@1 提升 4.2 个百分点；4) 与全索引音频检索相比，跨轮证据缓存在"上一轮检索正确且本轮目标为该证据一跳相邻块"的场景下显著减少检索错误。

Conclusion: 该论文的结论是：DUPAR 通过音频-文本联合嵌入、快慢双路径互补以及基于一跳图扩展的跨轮证据缓存机制，实现了语音直接检索在精度、速度与鲁棒性上的平衡——在干净语音上逼近文本检索精度并大幅降低延迟，在噪声与多说话风格下显著优于级联方案，且多轮对话中的缓存复用进一步降低了相邻话题检索的错误率，为基于外部知识的语音助手提供了一种更高效可靠的对话式检索框架。

Abstract: Voice assistants grounded in external knowledge typically use automatic speech recognition (ASR) to transcribe speech queries before retrieving evidence from textual knowledge bases. This cascade adds latency and propagates recognition errors, whereas direct speech retrieval is vulnerable to cross-modal misalignment. To address these limitations, we propose DUPAR, a conversational retrieval framework with complementary slow and fast paths. The fast path uses a task-adapted audio encoder aligned with frozen BGE-M3 text embeddings to search a cross-turn evidence cache. When cache confidence is insufficient, the slow path fuses full-index retrieval using audio and ASR-transcript embeddings, and the selected evidence refreshes the next-turn evidence cache through one-hop graph expansion. On a domain-specific knowledge base, our trained audio encoder approaches text-retrieval accuracy on clean speech with a 3.75$\times$ query-side speedup over ASR + Text Encoder. It raises average Recall@10 from 0.771 to 0.875 on the noise benchmark and improves overall Recall@1 by 4.2 percentage points across synthesized speaking styles. Compared with full-index audio retrieval, cross-turn evidence caching significantly reduces retrieval errors when the previous turn retrieves correct evidence and the follow-up targets a one-hop neighboring chunk.

</details>


### [174] [PageRecall: Measuring Page Selection in Literature-Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18154)
*Aaditya Chauhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是 LitTraceQA（GroundLM @ EMNLP 2026）的系统报告：给定研究问题，从 27,487 篇论文池中检索相关论文，引用答案所在的页码及表格/图并按要求格式作答。核心发现是"证据定位的瓶颈在检索而非阅读"：页面选择器仅有 52.6% 的金页召回率，而证据定位模型在拿到页面后引用正确率高达 94%（45/48）。据此，作者放弃页面选择、直接将整篇论文放入模型上下文，使可解析论文的金页召回率达 100%；对按位置定位目标的问题（如"第24条参考文献的第一作者"）改用参考文献解析。最终系统在留出测试集上取得 0.762 论文 F1、0.441 证据 F1 与 0.920 多选题准确率，并发布验证工具以复核论文核心主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献问答系统在引用证据（页码、表格或图）时，失败往往被归因于模型的阅读或定位能力。本文旨在构建一个面向 27,487 篇论文池的检索-引用-作答系统，并实证诊断证据接地的真正瓶颈所在——是模型"看不到"正确页面（检索问题），还是"看到了却找不准"（阅读问题），从而指导系统设计。

Method: 系统采用"问题→论文检索→页面选择→证据定位→格式化作答"的流水线。诊断实验揭示页面选择器是瓶颈后，方法上做出两个关键改变：(1) 放弃页面选择，将可完整解析的整篇论文直接送入模型上下文，页面排序仅作为超长论文（测试集并无此类论文）的回退方案；(2) 针对按位置而非内容定位目标的问题（如"第24条参考文献的第一作者"），将参考文献解析为可寻址列表，其生成的标识符还可作为证据指标的评分对象。

Result: 关键诊断结果：页面选择器的金页召回率仅 52.6%，而证据定位器在给定页面时 48 次输出中有 45 次引用正确（94%），证明瓶颈在检索而非阅读。失败模式分析显示：45 个页面缺失案例中，14 次无输出、24 次给出错误页面、仅 7 次碰巧正确，即静默失败（错误页面）约为可见失败（无输出）的两倍。改为展示全文后，可解析论文的金页召回率达 100%。最终系统在留出测试集上取得 0.762 论文 F1、0.441 证据 F1 和 0.920 多选题准确率。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：证据接地的瓶颈在检索端而非阅读端，工程上应当尽量消除"选择"环节——只要文档能放进上下文就直接展示全文，页面排序仅作为超长文档的兜底机制。同时，按位置引用的问题应通过解析（而非检索）来服务。由于系统依赖无种子控制的闭源模型，作者发布了一套验证工具（harness），通过提交的工件来复核论文的核心主张，以保障结果的可核查性与可复现性。

Abstract: We describe our system for LitTraceQA (GroundLM @ EMNLP 2026): given a research question, retrieve the relevant papers from a pool of 27,487, cite the page and the table or figure where the answer lives, and answer in a requested format. Our main finding is that evidence grounding is limited by retrieval, not by reading. The page selector put the annotator's page, which we call the gold page, in front of the model that locates evidence only about half the time (52.6% gold-page recall), while that model, given the page, cited the right one in 45 of the 48 locators it emitted (94%). When the page was missing it rarely said so: of 45 such cases it returned nothing 14 times, a wrong page 24 times, and a correct page 7 times, so the pipeline failed quietly almost twice as often as it failed visibly. Since the failure was that the right page was never shown, the fix is to stop choosing: each retrieved paper fits in the model's context, so we show it whole. Page ranking survives only as a fallback inside papers too long to fit, which no test-split paper was, and gold-page recall reaches 100% on the papers we can parse. Separately, questions that identify their target by position rather than content, such as "the first author of the 24th reference", are served by parsing rather than retrieval: we resolve the bibliography into an addressable list, which also supplies identifiers the evidence metric scores. The final system scores 0.762 paper $F_1$, 0.441 evidence $F_1$ and 0.920 multiple-choice accuracy on the held-out test split. Because the pipeline depends on a closed model without seed control, we release a harness that verifies the paper's central claims against committed artifacts.

</details>


### [175] [Single-Token Expected-Value Scoring for Cold-Start Candidate Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18188)
*Qihang Wang,Jinwei Tan,Mengyuan Shi,Mayank Sharma,Shuai Zhao,Fuxian Li,Ryan Yan,Alexander P. Kreuzer,Mohit Jain,Dheeraj Toshniwal,Manoj Seethamsetty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"单token期望值打分"这一排序原语，将候选人-职位相关性建模为对等级token {1,...,5} 的序数分类，并以首个token概率分布的期望作为相关性分数。作者用MSE+交叉熵的混合序数回归损失微调一个小语言模型（SLM），仅凭几十万条序数相关性标签即可实现确定性、低延迟的高质量招聘排序。离线实验、端到端仿真（求职者NDCG@10 +54.2%、低相关率 -46.7%）与真实在线实验（雇主低相关率 -27.3%、保留率 +7.07%）均证明其显著优于启发式基线与零样本LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助寻源能简化候选人审阅、降低招聘官人工筛选的管理负担，但将语言模型部署为生产级排序器仍具挑战：零样本LLM可能产生不稳定、非确定性的分数且排序精度欠佳；而传统深度神经排序器需要数百万条日志交互数据，低流量的细分招聘平台无法提供。平台实际拥有的仅是几十万条序数相关性标签——按排序器训练标准属于小规模数据，但预训练语言模型已编码该任务所依赖的通用世界知识，因此这批少量序数标签足以支撑有效的监督微调。

Method: 提出"单token期望值打分"（single-token expected-value scoring）排序原语：将候选人-职位相关性建模为等级token {1,...,5} 上的序数分类，相关性分数读取为首个token概率分布的期望值。由于分数来自单步解码而非开放式生成，它是模型logits的确定性函数，无需输出解析，且能以低延迟服务。为仅凭此类监督学习异构招聘标准之间的非线性相互依赖，作者用混合序数回归损失微调一个小语言模型（SLM）：该损失由保留序数距离信息的均方误差（MSE）项与锐化类别边界的类别交叉熵（Cross-Entropy）项组合而成。

Result: 在求职者相关性与雇主相关性两个维度上，以NDCG@10和低相关率进行评估。离线实验中，微调后的模型优于启发式基线与零样本LLM。端到端仿真呈现同方向且更大规模的提升：求职者NDCG@10提升54.2%，低相关率下降46.7%；真实在线实验将雇主侧低相关率降低27.3%，雇主保留率提升7.07%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：在标签量有限、流量稀疏、无法积累海量点击日志的垂直招聘场景中，依托预训练SLM的世界知识先验，以"单token期望值打分"结合MSE+交叉熵混合序数回归损失微调，即可获得确定性强、延迟低、排序精度高的生产级排序器。它有效弥合了零样本LLM（不稳定、精度不足）与传统深度神经排序器（数据饥渴）之间的空白，为低资源平台的AI排序落地提供了可复用的工程范式，且离线、仿真与真实在线三重实验均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: AI-assisted sourcing streamlines candidate review, reducing the administrative burden of manual screening for recruiters. However, deploying language models as production rankers remains challenging. Zero-shot Large Language Models (LLMs) may produce unstable, non-deterministic scores and rank less accurately, while conventional deep neural rankers require millions of logged interactions that a low-traffic, niche sourcing platform does not produce. What is available instead is a few hundred thousand ordinal relevance labels -- small by ranker-training standards, but sufficient when a pretrained language model already encodes the general world knowledge the task depends on.
  We present single-token expected-value scoring, a ranking primitive that casts candidate-job relevance as an ordinal classification over the grade tokens {1, ..., 5} and reads the relevance score as the expectation of the first-token probability distribution. Because the score comes from a single decoding step rather than open-ended generation, it is a deterministic function of the model's logits, requires no output parsing, and serves at low latency. To learn the non-linear interdependencies of heterogeneous hiring criteria from this supervision alone, we fine-tune a Small Language Model (SLM) with a hybrid ordinal regression loss combining a Mean Squared Error term, which preserves ordinal distance, with a categorical Cross-Entropy term, which sharpens class boundaries.
  We evaluate along two dimensions -- Jobseeker Relevance and Employer Relevance -- using NDCG@10 and low relevance rate. Offline, our fine-tuned model outperforms a heuristic baseline and zero-shot LLMs. An end-to-end simulation shows the same direction at larger magnitude (+54.2% Jobseeker NDCG@10, -46.7% low relevance rate), and a live online experiment reduces employer low-relevance by 27.3% and raises employer keep rate by 7.07%.

</details>


### [176] [Quanta: A Self-Contained Python Library for Hybrid Retrieval over Quantised Embeddings, Lexical Indexes, and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18248)
*Ioannis E. Livieris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出开源 Python 库 Quanta,将基于 4-bit 量化嵌入的稠密向量检索、BM25 全文检索与知识图谱遍历统一到单一检索 API 之下。其核心创新在于两点设计承诺:(1)采用加权倒数排名融合(RRF)组合多路检索信号,而非对异构分数做归一化(作者论证该归一化因依赖查询而是病态的);(2)知识图谱仅作为"候选扩展器"而非"相关性打分器",由稠密索引对扩展后的候选重新打分,实现"结构决定考虑什么、内容决定如何排序"的职责分离,从而显著简化 RAG 检索栈的部署与集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进的检索增强生成(RAG)流水线通常由三到四个独立运行的系统拼装而成:近似最近邻(ANN)索引、全文搜索引擎、图数据库和关系型文档存储。每个系统都带来各自的部署界面、配置模型与故障模式,而将它们粘合在一起的集成逻辑还需在每个项目中重新编写。此外,融合异构检索信号时常用的分数归一化方法依赖于具体查询,在方法论上并不成立(病态问题)。因此需要一种统一、健壮且融合原理更清晰的混合检索方案。

Method: Quanta 是一个开源 Python 库,在单一检索 API 之下统一三种检索能力:(1)基于 4-bit 量化嵌入的稠密向量搜索;(2)BM25 全文检索;(3)知识图谱遍历。其两项区别于现有混合检索栈的核心设计承诺为:第一,采用加权倒数排名融合来组合多路检索信号,而非将异构分数归一化到同一区间——作者论证这类归一化因依赖于具体查询而在方法论上是病态的;第二,图谱仅作为"候选扩展器"而非"相关性打分器"——图遍历只负责扩大候选池,新纳入的文档在标识符允许列表的约束下由稠密索引重新打分,即"结构邻近性决定考虑什么,内容证据决定如何排序"。

Result: 该摘要未报告具体实验数据或定量评估结果,其呈现的贡献主要是系统设计与架构层面的:Quanta 以单一 API 和上述两项设计承诺(加权 RRF 融合、图谱仅作候选扩展)区别于现有混合检索栈,从而降低多系统拼装带来的部署与集成开销。实际检索质量与效率的提升仍需依赖正文中的实验评估部分加以验证。

Conclusion: Quanta 论证了两个关键设计原则:其一,基于排名而非归一化分数的加权 RRF 融合,可从根本上规避异构分数归一化这一病态问题;其二,将知识图谱严格限定为候选扩展器、由稠密索引基于内容证据完成重打分,既保留了结构邻接信息"拓宽视野"的价值,又避免结构信号直接污染相关性排序。该方案为 RAG 检索栈提供了更简洁、可复用且原理更健全的统一实现。

Abstract: An advanced retrieval-augmented generation pipeline is typically assembled from three or four independently operated systems: an approximate nearest-neighbour index, a full-text search engine, a graph database, and a relational document store. Each contributes its own deployment surface, configuration model, and failure modes, and the integration logic that binds them is written anew in every project. In this work, we present \textsc{Quanta}, an open-source Python library, which unifies dense vector search over 4-bit quantised embeddings, BM25 full-text retrieval, and knowledge-graph traversal behind a single retrieval API. Quanta makes two design commitments, which distinguish it from existing hybrid retrieval stacks. First, signals are combined by \emph{weighted reciprocal rank fusion} rather than by normalising heterogeneous scores onto a shared range, which we argue is ill-posed because such normalisations are query-dependent. Second, the graph is a \emph{candidate expander and not a relevance scorer}: traversal widens the candidate pool, and the newly admitted documents are re-scored by the dense indexes under an identifier allowlist, so structural adjacency determines what is considered while content evidence determines how it ranks.

</details>


### [177] [One-Step Retrieval Framework for Real-Time Sponsored Search Ads Using Hierarchical Text Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18296)
*Tongtong Liu,Renyu Zhang,Jiayu Ding,Hongchao Guo,Xintao Yang,He Wei,Zhaoyu Li,Haiyang Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ANGLE（统一生成-判别-排序实时检索）框架，将广告的检索、相关性判别与排序直接集成到单一LLM中，并采用LLM生成的层级化文本表示（商业意图+广告摘要）替代传统离散语义标识符（SID），克服了多阶段级联架构目标不一致及SID方案泛化差、解码低效等问题。在实际搜索广告场景中，消耗提升1.81%、GMV提升2.16%，离线评估在HR、ACR等指标上全面超越七个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机源于现有两类方案的缺陷：(1) 传统检索系统采用多阶段级联架构（MCA），各模块独立优化，导致目标不一致，且高潜力候选广告在早期阶段即被错误淘汰；(2) 近期基于LLM的生成式方法虽提供端到端方案，但依赖基础LLM未曾学习的离散语义标识符（SID），SFT阶段需记忆大量SID到广告的映射，导致对未见广告泛化能力差、维护与更新成本高；SID与广告的一一映射还造成解码效率低下；此外，这些方法依赖小规模奖励模型（如pctr）进行相关性与排序，限制了LLM充分评估广告商业价值的能力。

Method: ANGLE框架的核心设计包含两点：(1) 层级化文本表示：利用LLM生成由"商业意图"（提供高层概览）与"广告摘要"（提供细粒度细节）组成的层级文本描述，替代离散SID，从而具备对未见广告的泛化能力并降低维护成本；(2) 统一建模：将检索、相关性判别与排序直接集成到单一LLM中，端到端地利用LLM的全部能力，实现广告的精准且高效的实时排序，避免小奖励模型的能力瓶颈。

Result: ANGLE在真实搜索场景中上线，消耗提升1.81%，商品交易总额（GMV）提升2.16%。在离线评估中，与七个基线方法对比，ANGLE在HR、ACR等关键指标上全面超越所有基线，验证了统一生成-判别-排序框架的优越性。

Conclusion: ANGLE通过将生成、判别与排序统一于单一LLM，并采用层级化文本表示替代离散语义标识符，系统性地解决了多阶段级联架构的目标不一致、SID方案泛化差与解码低效、以及商业价值评估受限等核心问题。在真实广告搜索场景中的显著在线收益与离线全面领先的表现，验证了该统一范式的有效性，为LLM驱动的生成式检索在工业界的落地提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Traditional retrieval systems typically use multi-stage cascading architectures (MCA), where each module is optimized independently, leading to inconsistent objectives and the premature elimination of high-potential candidates. Recent LLM-based generation methods offer end-to-end solutions but use discrete semantic identifiers (SIDs) to retrieve ads, which are not learned by the base LLM and require memorization of numerous SID-to-ad mappings during SFT, suffering from limited generalization to unseen ads, high maintenance and update costs. The one-to-one mapping between SIDs and advertisements leads to inefficient decoding. Moreover, these methods rely on a small reward model (e.g. pctr) for relevance and ranking, limiting the LLM's ability to fully assess ads' commercial value. To address these challenges, we propose A uNified Generation-discriminative-ranking reaL-time rEtrieval (ANGLE) framework. ANGLE uses LLM-generated hierarchical textual representations, which consist of commercial intent that provide high-level overviews and ad abstract that deliver fine-grained details. Additionally, ANGLE integrates retrieval, relevance, and ranking directly within a single LLM, enabling precise and efficient ranking of ads by leveraging the full capabilities of the LLM. We applied ANGLE to the real-world search scenarios, achieving a 1.81% increase in consumption and a 2.16% increase in gross merchandise volume (GMV). We also conducted offline evaluations of ANGLE and seven baselines, with ANGLE outperforming all across key metrics such as HR and ACR.

</details>


### [178] [Exploring LLMs and RAG for Plausible and Explainable Material Prediction of Vehicle Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18437)
*Frederik Wagner,Annerose Eichel,Sabine Schulte im Walde*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究 LLM 能否在无需大规模微调的情况下预测并解释车辆部件（如刹车盘、喷油器）的合理材料。作者比较了标准生成式 LLM、单次 RAG 和迭代式 CoVe 三种方法，并开发了网页专家标注工具用于评估。结果显示生成式 LLM 显著优于以往工作，但 RAG 方法未能进一步超越；同时指出超参数优化、高质量合法领域语料获取和专家评估设计等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏金标准、领域语料难以获取、预测结果难以可靠评估，是材料预测任务中的核心难题。作者希望探索 LLM 能否在无需大量微调的情况下准确预测并解释车辆部件（如制动盘、喷油器）的合理材料。

Method: 测试并对比三种方法：标准生成式 LLM 基线、单次检索增强生成（RAG）、迭代式验证链（CoVe）变体；检索基于公开可用的领域过滤维基百科语料。由于缺乏金标准，团队还开发了一个定制网页标注工具，支持领域专家进行结构化评估。

Result: 基于 LLM 的生成方法显著优于已有工作，但所测试的 RAG 方法未能进一步提升性能。研究同时揭示了 RAG 系统的现存挑战：超参数优化、高质量且合法可用的领域语料的可得性、以及专家评估实验的设计。

Conclusion: RAG 在材料预测任务上未带来超越基线的收益，且其落地面临高质量领域语料获取、检索参数调优与专家评测设计三大挑战；该研究同时提供了该领域的首个专家标注评测工具与基准。

Abstract: In this work, we explore whether LLMs can accurately predict and explain plausible materials for vehicle components such as brake discs or fuel injectors without requiring extensive fine-tuning. We test and evaluate three approaches: a standard generative LLM baseline, a single-pass Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) approach, and an iterative Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) variant. For retrieval, we rely on publicly available data using a domain-filtered Wikipedia corpus. Since no gold standard exists for this task, we develop a custom web-based annotation tool supporting crucial functions for structured domain expert evaluation. LLM-based generation substantially outperforms prior work, which is not further surpassed by the tested RAG approaches. Our results surface remaining challenges for RAG-based systems: hyperparameter optimization, the availability of high-quality, legally accessible domain corpora, and expert evaluation study design.

</details>


### [179] [SURF: Subtractive Updates for Recommender Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18695)
*Filippo Betello,Antonio Purificato,Nicola Tonellotto,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SURF框架，针对序列推荐系统（SRS）的机器遗忘问题，通过"邻域识别—局部辅助模型训练—推理时分数相减"的三阶段流程，以约2%的重训练时间成本实现与全量重训练相当的遗忘效果，并在NDCG@20上最高提升32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户隐私保护需求的增长以及GDPR等法规的合规要求，机器遗忘已成为现代推荐系统的基本能力。然而，序列推荐系统因依赖用户行为的时间交互模式，在遗忘任务上面临独特挑战：现有方法要么需要计算代价极高的全量重训练，要么忽略了用户行为的序列性，无法在效率与效果之间取得平衡。

Method: SURF（Subtractive Updates for Recommender Forgetting）框架分三阶段运作：(i) 在嵌入空间中定位待遗忘物品的邻域，提取局部子集；(ii) 仅在该紧凑子集上训练一个辅助模型；(iii) 推理阶段从原始模型分数中减去辅助模型的分数，实现减法式的遗忘更新，无需触碰整体模型参数。

Result: 在7个数据集上与5个基线方法的对比实验显示，SURF的遗忘效果与全量重训练相当，同时显著降低计算成本：NDCG@20最高提升32%，且仅需原始重训练基线约2%的时间预算，实现了效率与有效性的双赢。

Conclusion: SURF验证了在序列推荐系统中通过局部化、减法式更新实现近似机器遗忘的可行性。该方法以极低的计算开销（约2%重训练时间）达到与全量重训练相当的遗忘效果，在隐私合规与计算效率之间取得良好平衡，为大规模推荐系统的数据删除需求提供了实用的解决方案。作者已开源代码以便复现与后续研究。

Abstract: The increasing demand for user privacy and compliance with regulations such as GDPR has made machine unlearning a fundamental requirement for modern recommender systems. However, Sequential Recommender Systems (SRS) pose unique challenges for unlearning due to their reliance on temporal interaction patterns. Existing approaches either require computationally prohibitive full retraining or fail to account for the sequential nature of user behavior. We propose SURF (Subtractive Updates for Recommender Forgetting), a lightweight framework for approximate machine unlearning in SRS. SURF operates in three stages: (i) identifying the neighborhood of the item to forget in the embedding space, (ii) training an auxiliary model on this compact local subset, and (iii) subtracting the auxiliary model's scores from the original model at inference time. Experiments against five baselines on 7 datasets show that SURF achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to full retraining while substantially reducing computational cost, yielding up to a 32% improvement in NDCG@20 while requiring just 2% of the original retraining baseline time budget. We share our code at https://github.com/FilippoBetello/SURF.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISCERN:一个用于模型更新晋升认证的序贯两层审计协议。核心洞察是一个支持恒等式——两模型的风险差只存在于二者预测不一致的输入上,而这些不一致无需标签即可观测。零标签层可仅凭未标注流量认证不一致率低于容忍度的良性更新;审计层仅对抽样不一致样本请求标签,并使用任意时点有效的置信序列保证有效性。理论给出匹配的 O(ρ²/ε²) 标签复杂度界,较不利用配对信息的审计器节省 1/ρ 标注成本,且保证可跨无界次晋升用一个错误预算复合。在785个更新对、14,000+重放审计流(含至1.4B参数模型的LoRA微调)上,错误覆盖率仅0.0002(名义5%),功效0.986且零误报,56%良性更新零标签即通过认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产模型不可避免地会经历重训练、微调、量化甚至供应商静默替换等更新,而每次更新都可能比其取代的旧模型更差。现有多数审计方法需要大量标签估计风险差,且未区分两模型一致与不一致的样本,浪费标注预算。本文的动机是将更新晋升严格形式化为"认证式成对风险差审计",并利用不一致样本免标签可观测这一结构特性,大幅降低审计的标注成本,同时保证统计有效性。

Method: 核心是支持恒等式:两模型间的风险差仅体现在它们预测不一致的输入上,而不一致在无标签时即可观测。基于此构建DISCERN序贯两层协议:(1) 零标签层——仅凭未标注流量,即可认证不一致率低于容忍度的良性更新;(2) 审计层——仅对抽样到的不一致样本请求标签,采用任意时点有效的置信序列,在每个停止时刻以及任意标签路由规则(包括对抗性评审者)下均保持有效。理论上证明了有限样本有效性与匹配的标签复杂度界 O(ρ²/ε²),相比不利用配对结构的审计器可证明节省 1/ρ 的标注成本,且该保证可在一个错误预算下跨无界次数的晋升序列复合。

Result: 在超过14,000条重放审计流、涵盖785个更新对(包括参数量至1.4B的语言模型LoRA微调)的实验中:错误覆盖率为0.0002(名义水平5%),功效达0.986且零误报;56%的良性更新在零标签条件下即通过认证,验证了理论标签复杂度节省在实践中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文结论是:将模型更新晋升问题形式化为认证式成对风险差审计是可行且高效的。DISCERN通过支持恒等式实现免标签认证,配合任意时点有效的置信序列提供严格统计保证,可在一个错误预算下跨无界多次更新序列复合使用;每次审计生成机器可校验的证据记录,为生产模型的上市后监控提供了可落地的认证基础设施。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对在低端 GPU（如 16 GB 显存的 NVIDIA T4）上部署 RAG 时出现的"压缩悖论"（神经压缩引入 KV 缓存竞争与预处理延迟反而得不偿失，而跳过压缩又导致长上下文 OOM），提出免训练的确定性调度策略 Tri-Metric Router。它基于三个 CPU 侧信号——空间复杂度 L、句法密度 ρ_key 和类符/形符比 TTR，在 Raw、Neural（LLMLingua-2）与 Lexical（BM25）三种流水线间选择，并以显存余量和延迟交叉点为硬件物理调度依据。在 LongBench qasper 上校准后（T4 交叉点约 4,332 词），该方法在分布外数据上实现 0% OOM、88.5±4.4% oracle 对齐率和 49.3% Combined F1，比始终开启的词法压缩高 5.2 分，且无额外显存或训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在商品级 GPU（如 NVIDIA T4，16 GB 显存）上部署检索增强生成（RAG）时，作者观察到一种实际失效模式，称之为"压缩悖论"：神经提示压缩会引入 KV 缓存竞争与预处理延迟，其开销可能超过生成阶段节省的时间；而若完全跳过压缩，长上下文又会触发显存耗尽（OOM）。在严格内存预算下将 vLLM 服务的 LLM 与基于 PyTorch 的压缩器共部署时，存在两种截然不同的失败机制。现有研究多依赖语义层面的自适应，缺乏面向硬件物理约束的调度依据，因此需要一种不增加训练与显存开销、又能兼顾安全与效率的路由机制。

Method: 提出 Tri-Metric Router——一个确定性、免训练的路由策略，可在三种流水线之间进行选择：Raw（不压缩）、Neural（LLMLingua-2 神经压缩）与 Lexical（BM25 词法压缩）。路由器仅使用三个 CPU 侧可计算信号：空间复杂度（L）、句法密度（ρ_key）和类符/形符比（TTR）。与以往仅基于语义的自适应方法不同，其调度信号是硬件物理性的，依据显存余量和延迟交叉点进行判定。阈值通过对 LongBench qasper 数据集进行性能剖析来校准，在 T4 上得到约 4,332 词的运行交叉点；作者强调其贡献在于这套校准方法论，而非某个硬件特定的常数。

Result: 在分布外保留数据集上，该方法实现 0% 的 OOM 失败率、88.5 ± 4.4% 的 oracle 对齐率，以及 49.3% 的 Combined F1；相比始终开启的词法压缩方案提升 5.2 个百分点，且不引入额外的显存或训练成本。

Conclusion: 本研究的核心结论是：在低显存商品GPU上部署RAG时，调度决策应基于硬件物理信号而非纯语义信息。Tri-Metric Router以可校准的阈值方法论，在不增加显存与训练成本的前提下，同时规避了OOM与压缩开销反向抵消收益这两类失败，实现了安全性与压缩质量的平衡，为资源受限环境下的提示压缩自适应部署提供了可移植的工程方案。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant.
